[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTED_BONE_BLOCK
Tooltip: Collection Item; RARE
The Enchanted Bone Block is [[Rarity|RARE]] item unlocked at [[Bone|Bone X (10)]]. To craft it, the player needs 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Bone|Enchanted Bones]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Bone X (10) ([[Bone]])|160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Enchanted Bone]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Bone]] x32 ×7 → Enchanted Bone Block|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bonemerang]]|None|5x [[Spirit Bone]] [[Enchanted Bone Block]] 10x Enchanted Bone Block 15x [[Enchanted String]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted String]] x5 ×3 + Enchanted Bone Block x5 ×2 + [[Spirit Bone]] x5 → [[Bonemerang]]|
|[[Bone Reaver]]|None|1x [[Spirit Sword]] 5x [[Spirit Bone]] [[Enchanted Bone Block]] 20x Enchanted Bone Block|Recipe: Enchanted Bone Block x20 + [[Spirit Bone]] x5 + [[Spirit Sword]] → [[Bone Reaver]]|
## Trivia
- While Mojang introduced the Bone Block in 1.10, the actual block used for this block is the Chiseled Quartz Block texture. This is because Hypixel SkyBlock was made for Minecraft version 1.8.9.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 30, 2020|Added Enchanted Bone Block.|
|May 28, 2024|0.20.2|Enchanted Bone Block can now be sold to merchants for 51,200 [[Coins]].|

Enchanted Diamond Block is a [[Rarity|RARE]] block unlocked at [[Diamond|Diamond VIII (8)]].
Enchanted Diamond Blocks sell for 204,800 [[Coins]], making them very valuable. The price converts to 8 [[Coins]] per diamond. This item is also a pretty stable source of trading through items as the price of these are constantly very close to the merchant value at the [[Bazaar]].
Crafting Enchanted Diamond Blocks is a common way to gain lots of [[Carpentry]] XP.

Tooltip: Minion Upgrade Collection Item; This item can be used as a minion upgrade for chicken minions.; Guarantees that each chicken will drop an egg after they spawn.; Can also be used to craft more basic pets.; RARE
An Enchanted Egg is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item unlocked at [[Raw Chicken|Raw Chicken V (5)]]. It can be used as a [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]] to generate [[Egg|Eggs]] or to craft basic [[Pets]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Chicken V (5) ([[Raw Chicken]])|144x [[Egg]]|Recipe: [[Egg]] x16 ×9 → Enchanted Egg|
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv9]|[[Blue Jay]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
## Usage
### [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]]
Enchanted Egg can be placed in [[Chicken Minion]]. It allows [[Chicken|Chickens]] to lay [[Egg|Eggs]] as they spawn. The [[Chicken Minion]] collects the [[Egg|Eggs]] like other items.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Cake]]|Raw Chicken VII (7) ([[Raw Chicken]])|[[Enchanted Egg]] 1x Enchanted Egg 2x [[Enchanted Sugar]] 3x [[Wheat]] 3x [[Milk Bucket]]|Recipe: [[Milk Bucket]] ×3 + [[Enchanted Sugar]] ×2 + Enchanted Egg + [[Wheat]] ×3 → [[Enchanted Cake]]|
|[[Super Enchanted Egg]]|Raw Chicken IX (9) ([[Raw Chicken]])|[[Enchanted Egg]] 144x Enchanted Egg|Recipe: Enchanted Egg x16 ×9 → [[Super Enchanted Egg]]|
|[[Seagull Shard]]|Mangrove Log VII (7) ([[Mangrove Log]])|16x [[Enchanted Feather]] [[Enchanted Egg]] 16x Enchanted Egg 32x [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]] x8 ×4 + [[Enchanted Feather]] x4 ×4 + Enchanted Egg x16 → [[Seagull Shard]]|
|[[Sparrow Shard]]|Fig Log VII (7) ([[Fig Log]])|[[Enchanted Egg]] 16x Enchanted Egg 16x [[Enchanted Feather]] 32x [[Enchanted Fig Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Fig Log]] x8 ×4 + [[Enchanted Feather]] x4 ×4 + Enchanted Egg x16 → [[Sparrow Shard]]|
|[[Hawk Shard]]|Helix Log VII (7) ([[Helix Log]])|16x [[Enchanted Feather]] [[Enchanted Egg]] 16x Enchanted Egg 32x [[Enchanted Helix Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Helix Log]] x8 ×4 + [[Enchanted Feather]] x4 ×4 + Enchanted Egg x16 → [[Hawk Shard]]|
|[[June Bug Shard]]|Ruby Veilshroom IV (4) ([[Ruby Veilshroom]])|4x [[Enchanted Ruby Veilshroom]] [[Enchanted Egg]] 16x Enchanted Egg 32x [[Enchanted Helix Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Helix Log]] x8 ×4 + [[Enchanted Ruby Veilshroom]] ×4 + Enchanted Egg x16 → [[June Bug Shard]]|
|[[Queen Bee Shard]]|Honeycomb VII (7) ([[Honeycomb]])|16x [[Enchanted Honeycomb]] [[Enchanted Egg]] 16x Enchanted Egg 64x [[Enchanted Helix Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Honeycomb]] x4 ×4 + [[Enchanted Helix Log]] x16 ×4 + Enchanted Egg x16 → [[Queen Bee Shard]]|
|[[Honey Badger Shard]]|Honeycomb VII (7) ([[Honeycomb]])|4x [[Enchanted Honeycomb]] [[Enchanted Egg]] 16x Enchanted Egg 32x [[Enchanted Helix Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Helix Log]] x8 ×4 + [[Enchanted Honeycomb]] ×4 + Enchanted Egg x16 → [[Honey Badger Shard]]|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) [[Orchid Mantis Pet]]|None|[[Enchanted Egg]] 1x Enchanted Egg 8x [[Floral Gelatin]]|Recipe: [[Floral Gelatin]] ×8 + Enchanted Egg → [[Orchid Mantis Pet]]|
## Bugs
- Enchanted Egg as a [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]] also adds an [[Egg]] to the drops of the [[Chicken|Chickens]]. This results in two [[Egg|Eggs]] being made in one cycle while the island is loaded, one on spawn and one on harvest. While the island is unloaded, only one [[Egg]] will be made per cycle.
- The [[Egg|Eggs]] laid by the [[Chicken|Chickens]] on spawn are affected by drop multipliers and can trigger upgrade effects (e.g. [[Diamond Spreading]], [[Corrupt Soil]], or [[Raw Soulflow]] production from [[Soulflow Engine]]).
- While the island is loaded, [[Corrupt Soil]] does not make corrupt drops in a [[Chicken Minion]] without an Enchanted Egg.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Enchanted Egg.|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Fixed the price of [[Enchanted Paper]] and Enchanted Egg.|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Fixed being able to throw Enchanted Eggs.|
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|[[Chicken Minion]] now collect eggs created by Enchanted Egg.|
|Feb 28, 2020|Fixed being able to use 2 Enchanted Eggs on the same Minion ([[Minions]]).|
|Feb 28, 2020|Fixed Enchanted Egg not working while offline.|

Tooltip: Collection Item; RARE
Enchanted Hay Bale is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item unlocked at [[Wheat|Wheat XI (11)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wheat XI (11) ([[Wheat]])|160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Enchanted Wheat]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Wheat]] x32 ×7 → Enchanted Hay Bale|
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Fly]]|3|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.75%* ([[Pests]])|
### Legacy Trades
[[Enchanted Hay Bale]] 8 Enchanted Hay Bale (equivalent to 204,800 (3,200 stacks) [[Wheat]]) can be purchased for 1 [[Tightly-Tied Hay Bale]] (equivalent to 186,624 (2,916 stacks) [[Wheat]]) in the [[Legacy Trades]] menu.
[[Enchanted Hay Bale]] 64 (1 stack) Enchanted Hay Bale (equivalent to 1,638,400 (25,600 stacks) [[Wheat]]) can be purchased for 8 [[Tightly-Tied Hay Bale]] (equivalent to 1,492,992 (23,328 stacks) [[Wheat]]) in the [[Legacy Trades]] menu.
In both cases, the amount of Wheat the player receives is approximately 109.7% of the original amount.
## Usage
### Composter Upgrades
Enchanted Hay Bale can be used for Composter Speed upgrades in the [[Composter]]. [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] 468 (7 stacks plus 20) Enchanted Hay Bale is needed to max out Composter Speed.

# Enchanted Hopper
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Iron Ingot Collection, Minion Modifiers, Hopper, Automated shipping items, Enchanted

Type: Minion ([[Minions]]) upgrade ([[Automated Shipping]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Iron Ingot IX (9) ([[Iron Ingot]])
Function: Sells items generated by the minion for 70% of their sell price when the minion is full.
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Budget Hopper]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]

[Shop]

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 128,800x (2,012 stacks plus 32 (128,800 in total)) [[Iron Ingot]] 8x [[Planks]]
Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from merchants.): 257,604 [[Coins]]
Raw Materials to upgrade: 128,000x (2,000 stacks (128,000 items total)) [[Iron Ingot]]
Material cost to upgrade: 256,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTED_HOPPER
Tooltip: Minion Shipping; This item can be placed inside any minion. Automatically sells generated items when the minion has no space.; Items are sold for 70% of their selling price.; RARE
The Enchanted Hopper is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Automated Shipping]] item for [[Minions]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Iron Ingot IX (9) ([[Iron Ingot]])|1x [[Budget Hopper]] 5x [[Enchanted Iron Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Block]] ×5 + [[Budget Hopper]] → Enchanted Hopper|
## Usage
Enchanted Hopper can be placed in all [[Minions]]. It makes the minion sell overfill items at 70% of their NPC sell price. The [[Coins]] can be collected by clicking the hopper in the minion's inventory, this will cause the [[Coins]] to be placed in the player's purse.
There is no maximum amount of [[Coins]] that an Enchanted Hopper can hold, other than the 64-bit float limit of 1.79769e+308 (179769313486231570814527423731704356798070567525844996598917476803157260780028538760589558632766878171540458953514382464234321326889464182768467546703537516986049910576551282076245490090389328944075868508455133942304583236903222948165808559332123348274797826204144723168738177180919299881250404026184124858368).
## Tips
- Collecting coins from an Enchanted Hopper (or [[Budget Hopper]]) will not grant collection or skill experience. For those, the minion will have to periodically be emptied manually, in which case [[Minion Storage|Minion Storages]] and a [[Super Compactor 3000]] or [[Dwarven Super Compactor]] are recommended to have to collect less frequently.
## Trivia
- When the Enchanted Hopper was nerfed from 90% to 70% of NPC sell price, there was a bug where Enchanted Hoppers placed in minions before the nerf would not receive the nerf. This was later fixed and now all Enchanted Hoppers sell items at 70% of NPC sell price.

Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; RARE
The Enchanted Rabbit Foot is a [[Rarity|RARE]] crafted item unlocked at [[Raw Rabbit|Raw Rabbit IV (4)]], when 1,000 (15 stacks plus 40) [[Raw Rabbit]] is collected.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Rabbit IV (4) ([[Raw Rabbit]])|160x [[Rabbit's Foot]]|Recipe: [[Rabbit's Foot]] x32 ×7 → Enchanted Rabbit Foot|
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv63]|[[Carrot King]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
## Usage
Putting an Enchanted Rabbit Foot in a [[Brewing Stand]] with [[Awkward Potion|Awkward Potions]] creates the third tier [[Rabbit Potion]], which is needed to create any higher tier potion. A [[Rabbit Potion|Rabbit VI Potion]] is required for the [[Romero and Juliette]] questline.
## Trivia
- Even though the regular item is "Rabbit's Foot" in the possessive, the enchanted version does not use the possessive ('s), and is just written "Enchanted Rabbit Foot."

|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Enchanted Rabbit Foot.|

# Enchanted Redstone Lamp
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Glowstone Dust Collection, Brewing ingredients, Blocks, Enchanted, Brewing, Tier II Enchanted Items
[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]
Type: Brewing Ingredient
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Glowstone Dust VI (6) ([[Glowstone Dust]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 30,720 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 20,480x (320 stacks (20,480 items total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]]) 5,120x (80 stacks (5,120 items total)) [[Glowstone Dust]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTED_REDSTONE_LAMP
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; RARE
The Enchanted Redstone Lamp is a [[Rarity|RARE]] Brewing Ingredient unlocked at [[Glowstone Dust|Glowstone Dust VI (6)]]. When used while [[Potions|Brewing]] a potion, it applies the effects of both [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] and [[Enchanted Glowstone]], increasing the level by 3 and causing the effect to last for 16 more minutes. It is also used to craft the [[Blazetekk™ Ham Radio]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Glowstone Dust VI (6) ([[Glowstone Dust]])|32x [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] x32 ×4 + [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]] x32 → Enchanted Redstone Lamp|
## Usage
### [[Potions|Brewing]]
Brewing Enchanted Redstone Lamp with a Potion increases its level by 3 and increases the duration by 16 minutes.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Blazetekk™ Ham Radio]]|Blaze Slayer VI (6) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|24x [[Inferno Vertex]] [[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]] 64x Enchanted Redstone Lamp 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] x16 ×6 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x12 ×2 + Enchanted Redstone Lamp x64 → [[Blazetekk™ Ham Radio]]|
## Tips
- Using an Enchanted Redstone Lamp on a Skill XP Boost potion will bring the potion up to level 3 and extend the duration of the buff based on [[Alchemy]] level (36 minutes plus alchemy bonuses), regardless of the initial level of the boost potion.
- Use an [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] beforehand if you wish to maximize the potion's duration, because the Enchanted Redstone Lamp will take up the potion duration modifier with an Enchanted Redstone Dust.

|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Enchanted Redstone Lamp.|

### Treasure Hunter
2x Enchanted Sugar Cane is a potential reward from the [[Treasure Hunter]]'s buried treasure.
## Usage
### Composter Upgrades
Enchanted Sugar Cane can be used for Fuel Cap upgrades in the [[Composter]]. [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] 903 (14 stacks plus 7) Enchanted Sugar Cane is needed to max out Fuel Cap.

### [[Speed Potion|Speed V Potion]]
Enchanted Sugar Cane is known to be one of the best sources of [[Alchemy]] skill, giving 15,000 XP per Speed V potion brewed. It is however quite expensive given the current bazaar prices. A cheaper, though much more time-consuming, is building an auto brewer and using enchanted melon to brew many Healing III potions instead. It is also a decent way to level up pets, being 1/12 as efficient on pets that aren't alchemy. (It was previously 1/4 like the other skills, but it got significantly nerfed.)
Recipe: Enchanted Sugar Cane → [[Speed Potion]], [[Speed Potion]], [[Speed Potion]]
### Merchants
|Name|Merchant|Requirements|Ingredients|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sugar Cane Minion]] XII|Tony's Shop ([[Tony]])|Sugar Cane I (1) ([[Sugar Cane]])|[[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] 32x Enchanted Sugar Cane 1x Sugar Cane Minion XI ([[Sugar Cane Minion]]) 75 [[Pelts]]|
## Trivia
- Enchanted Sugar Cane, Enchanted Gold Block and Enchanted Fermented Spider Eye gives the same amount of [[Alchemy|Alchemy XP]] when brewed as potion.

# End Portal Fumes
Categories: Rare items, Items, Mixins, Brewing ingredients, Brewing, Potions, Slayer
[End Portal Fumes]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Enderman Slayer VIII (8) ([[Enderman Slayer]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Bartender]]
Buy: 150,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: END_PORTAL_FUMES_MIXIN
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; ⸎ Soulflow conversions provide +30% more  Overflow.; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active God Potion); ☠ Requires Enderman Slayer 8.; RARE
End Portal Fumes is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
### Purchase
End Portal Fumes can be purchased from the [[Bartender]] for 150,000 [[Coins]] once the player reaches [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer VIII (8)]].
## Usage
### Brewing
End Portal Fumes can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to make [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] conversions provide +30% more [[Mana|Overflow Mana]]. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: End Portal Fumes → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## Trivia
- End Portal Fumes is the only [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that does not contain the word 'Mixin' in its name.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 21, 2021|0.11.4|Added End Portal Fumes.|

# End Stone Geode
Aliases: Endstone Geode, Hyper
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Items, Reforging
[End Stone Geode]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])

[Reforge/Power Stone]

Applied To: [[Armor]]
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENDSTONE_GEODE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Hyper reforge when combined with Armor.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Attack Speed  Speed; Hyper Bonus Grants +5 Speed for 5s after using a Transmission ability.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 14.; RARE REFORGE STONE
The End Stone Geode is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Hyper]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]], granting [[Strength]], [[Attack Speed]], and [[Speed]].
## Obtaining
The End Stone Geode has a 1.43% (1 in 70.07) chance to drop from mining [[End Stone]] [[Ender Nodes]] in [[The End]].
## Usage
### Reforging
The End Stone Geode can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Hyper]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], [[Attack Speed]], and [[Speed]], which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XIV (14)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Hyper]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Hyper Bonus, which grants 5 [[Speed]] for 5 seconds after teleporting using a [[Transmission (disambiguation)|Transmission]] ability.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+2 Str ([[Strength]]) +2 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +1 Spd ([[Speed]])|+4 Str ([[Strength]]) +3 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +1 Spd ([[Speed]])|+6 Str ([[Strength]]) +4 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +2 Spd ([[Speed]])|+7 Str ([[Strength]]) +5 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +2 Spd ([[Speed]])|+10 Str ([[Strength]]) +6 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +3 Spd ([[Speed]])|+12 Str ([[Strength]]) +7 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +3 Spd ([[Speed]])|+14 Str ([[Strength]]) +8 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +4 Spd ([[Speed]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Hyper Bonus; Gain +5 Speed for 5s after using a Transmission ability.|Hyper Bonus; Gain +5 Speed for 5s after using a Transmission ability.|Hyper Bonus; Gain +5 Speed for 5s after using a Transmission ability.|Hyper Bonus; Gain +5 Speed for 5s after using a Transmission ability.|Hyper Bonus; Gain +5 Speed for 5s after using a Transmission ability.|Hyper Bonus; Gain +5 Speed for 5s after using a Transmission ability.|Hyper Bonus; Gain +5 Speed for 5s after using a Transmission ability.|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The End Stone Geode originally applied the Warped reforge. It was renamed to [[Reforging|Hyper]] to avoid confusion with the [[Warped Stone]].

The [[Zealot|Zealots]] have shown me so much love, I hope this simple rose shows how much I care for them.
— End Stone Protector
An End Stone Rose is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item dropped by [[End Stone Protector|End Stone Protectors]].
## Obtaining
An End Stone Rose is a guaranteed drop from killing the [[End Stone Protector]] in [[The End]].
## Usage
[[Pablo]] has a 10% chance to request an End Stone Rose in exchange for [[Factions|25 ቾ Mage Reputation]].
## Trivia
- The End Stone Rose references [[Iron Golem|Iron Golems]] offering a [[Flowers|Poppy]] to villagers and dropping them on death in Vanilla Minecraft.
- The End Stone Rose replaced the previous [[Flowers|Poppy]] drop for the purpose of being sold to merchants for 3,000 [[Coins]].

# Entangler Lasso
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Confirmations needed, Crafting ingredients, Vinesap Collection, Tools
[Entangler Lasso]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Vinerip Lasso]]
Next →: [[Everstretch Lasso]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENTANGLER_LASSO
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Ability: Throw RIGHT CLICK Throw the Lasso at Forest creatures and hold them still until they lose all their stamina.; Ability: Reel In RIGHT CLICK Reel in creatures caught by the Lasso each time their stamina depletes.;  Critters lose stamina 40% faster.; Ability: Tug Grants +2.5 Hunting Fortune on Forest Shards caught with this Lasso.; RARE LASSO
An Entangler Lasso is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Lassos|Lasso]], whose recipe is unlocked at [[Vinesap|Vinesap IV (4)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Vinesap IV (4) ([[Vinesap]])|1x [[Mangcore]] 1x [[Vinerip Lasso]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Vinesap]]|Recipe: [[Vinesap]] x32 ×3 + [[Vinerip Lasso]] + [[Mangcore]] → Entangler Lasso|
## Usage
An Entangler Lasso can be thrown at forest creatures to catch them. While held, a stamina bar appears above its head. When the bar reaches 20%[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], a prompt to reel appears. Right-clicking will deplete the stamina bar and pull in the mob.
An Entangler Lasso depletes stamina bars on [[Critters|Critter]] type mobs 40% faster.
An Entangler Lasso also grants +2.5 [[Hunting Fortune]] on Forest shards caught with this lasso.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Everstretch Lasso]]|Vinesap VI (6) ([[Vinesap]])|[[Entangler Lasso]] 1x Entangler Lasso 3x [[Enchanted Vinesap]] 8x [[Mangcore]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Vinesap]] ×3 + Entangler Lasso + [[Mangcore]] x8 → [[Everstretch Lasso]]|

# Experimentation Table
Aliases: Experiment Table
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Pages with UIs
[Experimentation Table]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 8x [[Oak Log]] 6x [[Lapis Lazuli]] 1x [[Glass Bottle]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EXPERIMENTATION_TABLE
Tooltip: Place this item on your island!; Conduct wild enchanting experiments to gain bonus Enchanting Exp and items!; RARE
The Experimentation Table is a [[Rarity|RARE]] piece of non-cosmetic [[Furniture]] that can be used daily where the player gets to play short games in exchange for [[Enchanting|Enchanting XP]], high level Enchanted Books, [[Endcaps|Endcap Enchantments]] and Experience Bottles. These games reset 24 hours after consuming the last charge. Extra charges purchasable with [[Bits]] reset every day at 12:00 AM GMT or 7:00 PM EST/8:00 PM EDT. The recipe is unlocked when the player reaches [[Enchanting|Enchanting X (10)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enchanting X (10) ([[Enchanting]])|1x [[Experience Bottle]] 8x [[Oak Log]]|Recipe: [[Oak Log]] ×8 + [[Experience Bottle]] → Experimentation Table|
## Usage
The Experimentation Table can be placed on a private island. A maximum of 1 table can be placed at a time.
Right clicking the table brings up a GUI where the player can do [[Experiments]]. Experiments are minigames that grant Enchanting XP and Enchanting-related rewards.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Gallery
- A placed down Experimentation Table
## History

|Aug 8, 2023|Fixed Experimentation Table still having duplicate Experience Bottle pairs.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Removed [[Piscary]] from the Experimentation Table.|

An Extremely Mild Adhesive is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Sticky]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]], which grants [[Pull]].
## Obtaining
An Extremely Mild Adhesive can be purchased from [[Miria]] for 100 [[Miria's Coupon]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Extremely Mild Adhesive can be combined with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Sticky]] reforge, to grant [[Pull]], which scales with the Fishing Net's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining X (10)]].
Additionally, it grants the Sticky Bonus, which grants 2.5 [[Hunting Fortune]] towards Water Shards.

# Extremely Real Shuriken
Aliases: Fake Shuriken, Shuriken
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Extremely Real Shuriken]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FAKE_SHURIKEN
Tooltip: Can be thrown at Enemies to tag them. Grants +5%  Magic Find when a tagged Enemy is killed.; Cooldown: 1s; X Rift-Transferable X RARE
An Extremely Real Shuriken is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
[[Extremely Real Shuriken]] 16 Extremely Real Shuriken can be obtained for 32 [[Sun Gecko Essence]] from [[Oruo The Almighty]].
## Usage
Item:
Extremely Real Shuriken
[Effects]
[Can be thrown at Enemies to tag them. Grants +5% Magic Find when a tagged Enemy is killed.; Cooldown: 1s]
An Extremely Real Shuriken can be thrown at an enemy to tag them, granting +5% [[Magic Find]] against the tagged enemy when killed.
Mobs tagged by an Extremely Real Shuriken will have a ✯ symbol at the end of their name tag.
## Bugs
- The [[Magic Find]] buff does not apply to mobs captured with a [[Black Holes|Pocket Black Hole]].
- The symbol that appears in an entity's nametag when tagged still uses the old "✯" unicode symbol instead of the new custom "" from the official resource pack
## Trivia
- The name Extremely Real Shuriken is likely meant to be sarcastic.
  - This is reinforced by the fact that the ID of the item is FAKE_SHURIKEN.
- When used against [[Slayer]] Bosses, an Extremely Real Shuriken gives an additional flat +5 [[Magic Find]] on top of the +5% [[Magic Find]].
  - This is essentially mathematically equivalent to increasing the drop chance of rare items by 5%, regardless of the player's base [[Magic Find]].
- Extremely Real Shurikens can be used as a substitute for [[Time Shuriken|Time Shurikens]] in the [[Sun Gecko]] fight.
- Extremely Real Shurikens cannot be used on [[The Garden]].

## Trivia
- Fairy Armor is one of two animated [[Armor]] sets that are in the game, the other being [[Great Spook Armor]]. ( [[Crystal Armor]] does not count as it only changes color based on light level).
- The maximum [[Health]] that can be gained from finding all the Fairy Souls, including the base 4 , is 293 .
- Players used to be able to dye other [[Armor|armor pieces]] with glitches involving this set.
- It alternates between various shades of purple, pink, red, white, and blue.
- Upon wearing a full set, the player will unlock the [[Achievements|Raaaiiinnnnbbooww!]] achievement.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:

|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Increased piece bonus from +5 [[Speed]] to +10 [[Speed]].|
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Full Set Bonus now gives +1 [[Health]] for every Fairy Soul ([[Fairy Souls]]) found.|
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|When you're near a Fairy Soul you have not found yet, a sound will play.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed Fairy Armor giving extra speed.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed Fairy Armor not playing a sound when Fairy Souls are nearby.|

[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: FALCON
Tooltip: Battle Experience I Pets gain +1% more EXP from Combat.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE FOREST SHARD (ID R58)
The Falcon Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Battle Experience]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Falcon Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: Bird Family
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Falcon Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Battle Experience]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Battle Experience ([[Attributes]]); Pets gain +1%–10% more EXP from Combat.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Falcon Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Falcon Shard has the following description:
- Peregrine Falcons, the fastest animals in the world, can reach diving speeds of over 386 kilometers per hour.

# Fallen Star Helmet
Categories: Rare items, Armor, Dwarven Mines
[Fallen Star Helmet]
Type: Helmet ([[Helmets]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 50; [[Defense]]: 30; [[Intelligence]]: 2
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Shop]
Sell: 500 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FALLEN_STAR_HAT
Tooltip: Health: +50; Defense: +30; Intelligence: +2; Wear this hat to a Cult of the Fallen Star meeting.; This item can be reforged!; * Co-op Soulbound * RARE HELMET
The Fallen Star Helmet is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Helmets|Helmet]].
## Obtaining
A Fallen Star Helmet can be obtained by talking to the [[Cult of the Fallen Star]] in their cave in the [[Cliffside Veins]]. The cult only appears between 12a.m. (Midnight, 00h, start of the 7th/14th/21st/28th day) until 6:00a.m. in in-game time every 7 SkyBlock days (7th, 14th, 21st, and 28th of each SkyBlock Month).
Warning: Closing the GUI while claiming the helmet currently stops you from getting it.
## Usage
Helmet:
Fallen Star Helmet [[Health]]: +50 [[Defense]]: +30 [[Intelligence]]: +2
[Effects]
[Wear this hat to a Cult of the Fallen Star meeting.]
If the player wears a Fallen Star Helmet during a [[Cult of the Fallen Star]] meeting (except for the one where they obtain it), they will receive 200 (3 stacks plus 8) [[Starfall]].
Wearing a Fallen Star Helmet while talking to [[Silnar]] near a [[Fallen Stars|Fallen Star]] will reward the player with a [[Fallen Star Lozenge]].
Wearing a Fallen Star Helmet is required to obtain the [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]] of [[Cult of the Fallen Star|Dalir]].
## Trivia
- Once unlocked, a Fallen Star Helmet can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] for 500,000 [[Coins]].
- Fallen Star Chestplate, Leggings, and Boots exist in the game, however they are unobtainable and are only used as [[Silnar]]'s armor.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Fallen Star Helmet.|
|Apr 25, 2022|Fallen Star Helmet was added to [[Rusty]]'s shop and is now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Jul 9, 2024|0.20.4|Fixed Fallen Star Helmet not showing up in Rusty's shop after meeting the requirements.|

A Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item used to attract [[Critters]]. It is unlocked at [[Honeycomb|Honeycomb III (3)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting

A Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb can be used to lather trees with an active [[Honey|Tree Protection Order]] on them. After 30m, a unique [[Critters|Critter]] will spawn depending on the type of tree lathered.

# Farming Toolkit
Aliases: Farmingtoolkit
Categories: Rare items, Items, Pages with UIs
[Farming Toolkit]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FARMING_TOOLKIT
Tooltip: Consumable; Store all of your Farming Tools in one convenient place and swap between them with ease.; Right-click to consume!; ❣ Requires Farming Skill 5.; RARE
A Farming Toolkit is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be consumed to unlock the Farming Toolkit menu.
## Obtaining
A Farming Toolkit can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] on [[The Garden]] for 500 [[Copper]].
## Usage
A Farming Toolkit can be consumed once to permanently unlock the Farming Toolkit menu and gain [[SkyBlock Levels|2 SkyBlock XP]].
The Farming Toolkit menu can be opened in the player's [[Storage]] or with the /farmingtoolkit command. It can store one of each [[Farming Tools|Specialized Farming Tool]].
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming the Farming Toolkit|You have unlocked the Farming Toolkit! Access it with /farmingtoolkit|
|Trying to consume a second Farming Toolkit|You have already unlocked this toolkit!|
|Running /farmingtoolkit without it unlocked|You haven't unlocked the Farming Toolkit yet!|
## History

|Apr 21, 2026|Added Farming Toolkit.|
|Apr 27, 2026|Decreased cost from 2,500 [[Copper]] to 500 [[Copper]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

The Firedust Dagger is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer II (2)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Slayer II (2) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Stick]] 1x [[Molten Powder]] 30x [[Silver Magmafish]]|Recipe: [[Silver Magmafish]] x30 + [[Molten Powder]] + [[Stick]] → Firedust Dagger|
## Usage
Sword:
Firedust Dagger [[Damage]]: +90 [[Strength]]: +45 [[Crit Damage]]: +15
[Effects]
[Deal 1.2x damage to Infernal (Infernal (Mob Type)) mobs.; Deal 1.1x damage to Wither mobs.; Gain +15 Combat Wisdom against Infernal (Infernal (Mob Type)) mobs. Ability: Attunement ON RIGHT CLICK; Swap between Ashen and Auric mode in order to attack through Hellion Shields. Ability: Burning Vengeance ﬗ; On hit, repeat the last 6 hits of dealt damage after 6s if the Hellion Shield has switched attunement.]
The Firedust Dagger deals 1.2x damage to [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs and 1.1x damage to [[Skeletal]] mobs. Additionally, it provides +15 [[Combat Wisdom]] against [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs.
The Firedust Dagger has 2 abilities:
- Attunement allows the wielder to swap between Ashen and Auric attunements in order to attack through the [[Inferno Demonlord]]'s Hellion Shield ability. When switching attunement, the Firedust Dagger changes appearance from a Iron Sword to a Golden Sword.
- Burning Vengeance allows the wielder to repeat the last 6 hits of dealt damage after 6s upon hit if the Hellion Shield has switched attunement. This does not affect the damage immunity of demons, and has a cap of 25,000,000 [[Damage]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Kindlebane Dagger]]|Blaze Slayer IV (4) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|[[Firedust Dagger]] 1x Firedust Dagger 1x [[Gold Magmafish]] 8x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Gold Magmafish]] + [[Molten Powder]] x8 + Firedust Dagger → [[Kindlebane Dagger]]|

# Fleshtrap
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[Fleshtrap]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FLESHTRAP
Tooltip: A twisted version of the Venus flytrap, this plant thrives on feeding its insatiable hunger for flesh. It requires regular offerings to stay alive, or it withers away.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; RARE MUTATION
A Fleshtrap is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Fleshtrap can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Cindershade]] and 4 [[Lonelily]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Fleshtrap needs water and has 14 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it allows for additional items to be found.
Fleshtraps require meat-based food items while growing. The player can feed it by right-clicking the Fleshtrap with the food item. Different types of food provide different effects:
- Basic food simply recovers 2 points of hunger.
- Enchanted food recovers 4 points of hunger, as well as adding a +20% bonus drop onto the Fleshtrap.
- Enchanted cooked food ([[Enchanted Cooked Mutton|Mutton]], [[Enchanted Cooked Rabbit|Rabbit]], etc.) recovers 6 points of hunger and adding a +40% bonus drop.
The bonus drop caps at +100%, though it still has a chance to give up to 3 Fleshtraps. It is not recommended to feed it enchanted cooked food as enchanted food is enough to reach the cap. Basic food can be fed after it reaches the cap in order to sustain it, as a Fleshtrap cannot grow if its hunger reaches 0.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Fleshtraps also drop 289 (4 stacks plus 33) [[Pumpkin]], 510 (7 stacks plus 62) [[Potato]], 595 (9 stacks plus 19) [[Carrot]], and +2,720 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Fleshtrap can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 360,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 180 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Fleshtrap grants an additional 750 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Fleshtraps are used to create a [[Zombud]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])|DNA Analysis Milestone V (5) ([[Crop Analyzer]])|1x Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]) 8x [[Cheesebite]] 8x [[Blastberry]] [[Fleshtrap]] 16x Fleshtrap|Recipe: Fleshtrap x4 ×4 + [[Blastberry]] x4 ×2 + [[Cheesebite]] x4 ×2 + [[Mutations Sack]] → [[Mutations Sack]]|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Feeding while full|[CROP] Fleshtrap: I'm already full!|
## Trivia
- Fleshtraps do not decay when fully grown or placed by the player.
## Gallery
- Fleshtrap in the Greenhouse Fleshtrap in the [[Greenhouse]]

# Flowering Bouquet

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FLOWERING_BOUQUET
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Blooming reforge when combined with Equipment.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Farming Fortune  Speed; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 13.; RARE REFORGE STONE
The Flowering Bouquet is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Blooming]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Equipment]], which grants [[Farming Fortune]] and [[Speed]].
## Obtaining
The Flowering Bouquet has a 2% (1/50) chance to be given by [[Garden Visitors]] in the [[The Garden]] when completing offers.
## Usage
### Reforging
Flowering Bouquet can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Blooming]] reforge, to grant [[Farming Fortune]] and [[Speed]], which scale with the equipment's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XIII (13)]] and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece. Equipment that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Blooming]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+4 Spd ([[Speed]]) +1 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+4 Spd ([[Speed]]) +2 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+5 Spd ([[Speed]]) +3 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+5 Spd ([[Speed]]) +4 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+6 Spd ([[Speed]]) +5 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+6 Spd ([[Speed]]) +6 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+7 Spd ([[Speed]]) +7 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
### Composter
Flowering Bouquet can be used in the [[Composter]] for 6,000 Organic Matter.
## Trivia
- Flowering Bouquet and [[Golden Ball]] are the only reforge stones that can be consumed for a purpose different than applying a reforge.

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Fluffling Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Foraging Tracker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Foraging Tracker ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Tracking]] while on Foraging Islands.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Tooltip: Catacombs Cap I Increases your  Speed Cap by +5; while in The Catacombs.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE COMBAT SHARD (ID R28)
The Folf Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Catacombs Cap]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Folf Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]], [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Soul of the Alpha Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Folf Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Catacombs Cap]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Catacombs Cap ([[Attributes]]); Increases your [[Speed]] Cap by +5-50 while in the The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]]).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Folf Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Folf Shard has the following description:
- The Folf is not particularly smart, agile, or anything else for that matter. It's smaller than a wolf, and dumber than a fox.

# Foul Flesh
Categories: Rare items, Minion fuels, Templates with deprecated argument names, Minion Modifiers, Slayer, Zombie Slayer, Brewing ingredients, Mob Drops
[Foul Flesh]
Type: [[Minion Fuel]]
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 25,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FOUL_FLESH
Tooltip: Minion Fuel Brewing Ingredient; Increases the speed of your minion by 90% for 5; hours!; RARE
Foul Flesh is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that is an [[Drop Chance|3Occasional]] drop from the [[Revenant Horror]].
## Obtaining
Foul Flesh is a [[Drop Chance|3Occasional]] drop from Tier II, Tier III, Tier IV and Tier V [[Revenant Horror|Revenant Horrors]].
Drop Chances:
- II: 16.16% (1x)
- III: 16.08% (1-2x)
- IV: 16.22% (2-3x)
- V: 13.77% (3-4x)
## Usage
### Minion Fuel
Foul Flesh can be used as a fuel source for [[Minions]]. It increases the efficiency by 90% for 5h. Foul Flesh can be placed in all [[Minions]].
### Brewing
It can be used as an ingredient to produce a [[Stamina Potion]] from [[Awkward Potion|Awkward Potions]]. Stamina Potions provide +150 [[Health]] and +50 [[Mana]] at level 1.
Recipe: Foul Flesh → [[Stamina Potion]], [[Stamina Potion]], [[Stamina Potion]]
### Abiphone Contact
[[Fred]] can be given a 64x Foul Flesh in exchange for their [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.
## Tips
- While there are more cost efficient fuels, Foul Flesh has the highest minion speed boost of all fuels. So if a player wants to collect generated resources manually, this can be their go to option.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Added Foul Flesh.|
|Apr 3, 2020|Added Foul Flesh to the [[Bazaar]].|

# Framed Volcanic Stonefish

Item ID: FRAMED_VOLCANIC_STONEFISH
Tooltip: * Soulbound * RARE
The Framed Volcanic Stonefish is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item used in the Mage [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]].
## Obtaining
The Framed Volcanic Stonefish can only be obtained while completing [[Odexar]]'s Quest. The player must bring Odexar's [[Trophy Fish|Volcanic Stonefish]] to [[Odger]], who tells them to fish up another Volcanic Stonefish. Once the player brings another Volcanic Stonefish to Odger, he will give the player a Framed Volcanic Stonefish.
## Usage
The Framed Volcanic Stonefish can be given to Odexar to complete their quest.
## Trivia
- If the player closes Odger's UI containing the Framed Volcanic Stonefish, they will have to give Odger another Volcanic Stonefish to reopen the UI.

Freshly-Minted Coins is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be purchased from [[Liz]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Freshly-Minted Coins can be bought from [[Liz]] for 1 [[Stock of Stonks]]. It has a global stock of [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] 5,000 (78 stacks plus 8) Freshly-Minted Coins: the stock resets every 4h.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Blood-Stained Coins]]|None|[[Freshly-Minted Coins]] 1x Freshly-Minted Coins 4x [[Hemoglass]]|Recipe: [[Hemoglass]] ×4 + Freshly-Minted Coins → [[Blood-Stained Coins]]|
|[[Relic of Coins]]|None|1x [[Artifact of Coins]] [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] 64x Freshly-Minted Coins|Recipe: Freshly-Minted Coins x16 ×4 + [[Artifact of Coins]] → [[Relic of Coins]]|
|[[Artifact of Coins]]|None|1x [[Ring of Coins]] [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] 16x Freshly-Minted Coins|Recipe: Freshly-Minted Coins x4 ×4 + [[Ring of Coins]] → [[Artifact of Coins]]|
|[[Ring of Coins]]|None|1x [[Talisman of Coins]] [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] 4x Freshly-Minted Coins|Recipe: Freshly-Minted Coins ×4 + [[Talisman of Coins]] → [[Ring of Coins]]|
|[[Scavenger Ring]]|None|1x [[Scavenger Talisman]] 4x [[Blood-Stained Coins]] [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] 8x Freshly-Minted Coins|Recipe: [[Blood-Stained Coins]] ×4 + Freshly-Minted Coins x2 ×4 + [[Scavenger Talisman]] → [[Scavenger Ring]]|
|[[Scavenger Artifact]]|None|1x [[Scavenger Ring]] 4x [[Blood-Soaked Coins]] [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] 16x Freshly-Minted Coins|Recipe: [[Blood-Soaked Coins]] ×4 + Freshly-Minted Coins x4 ×4 + [[Scavenger Ring]] → [[Scavenger Artifact]]|
## Trivia
- The verb "to mint" originates from the Latin "monēta" meaning coined money, it's a cognate with the word money.
- Total of [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] 116 (1 stack plus 52) Freshly-Minted Coins are required to max both of the accessories.

# Frozen Bait

[Frozen Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])

[Shop]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FROZEN_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Grants +50 Fishing Speed Grants +8 Sea Creature Chance; Can only be used in Jerry's Workshop.; RARE BAIT
The Frozen Bait is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Bait]].
## Obtaining
Frozen Bait has a 0.72% chance to appear as [[Glacial Cave|Frozen Treasure]].
Frozen Bait can also be obtained by completing various Gift Milestones from [[Generow]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Frozen Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Frozen Bait will be consumed to grant +50 [[Fishing Speed]] and +8 [[Sea Creature Chance]] in the [[Jerry's Workshop]]. Note that the [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]] don't show up in the player's stats. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.

## Trivia
- The Frozen Scythe is a reference to the Snowman Kit in Mega Walls.
- If the weapon's Ability is used right behind a 1-block thick wall, the ice bolt will actually be launched from the other side of the wall, which means mobs can be attacked behind it.

# Fruit Bowl
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Fruit Bowl]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FRUIT_BOWL
Tooltip: Consumable; Click on a player while holding this item to add the name of their profile to this item.; Find one of each default profile name to reset this item and permanently gain +1☯ Farming Wisdom and 250,000 Coins.; Names missing:; Apple, Banana, Blueberry, Cucumber, Coconut, Grapes, Kiwi, Lemon, Lime, Mango, Orange, Papaya, Pineapple, Peach, Pear, Pomegranate, Raspberry, Strawberry, Tomato, Watermelon, Zucchini; You may only gain the bonus once!; RARE
The Fruit Bowl is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
A Fruit Bowl has a 0.2% (1 in 500) chance to be rewarded from completing a [[The Garden|Garden]] visitor offer.
Fruit Bowl can also be obtained as a Minor Reward during [[Raffle of the Century]] event, or from the [[Gift Bag of the Century]].
## Usage
The player can click on any other player while holding a Fruit Bowl to add the name of their profile to the item.
FRUIT BOWL! <player>'s profile name is <profile>!
<profile> has been added to the fruit bowl!
If the profile name has already been found, the result will be:
FRUIT BOWL! <player>'s profile name is <profile>!
You already found this profile name in your bowl!
Finding each default profile name will reset the item and grant the player a permanent +1 [[Farming Wisdom]] and 250,000 [[Coins]]. The player may only gain the bonus once.
FRUITALICIOUS! You completed your fruit bowl and gained its permanent bonus!
That also comes with a one-time reward of 250,000 Coins!
The bonus is a one time reward. If the player finds all profile names again they will receive nothing but a message in chat.
FRUITALICIOUS! You completed your fruit bowl!
You had already gained the bonus, so... at least everyone knows you love fruits now!
All default profile names can be seen below.
- Apple
- Banana
- Blueberry
- Coconut
- Cucumber
- Grapes
- Kiwi
- Lemon
- Lime
- Mango
- Orange
- Papaya
- Pear
- Peach
- Pineapple
- Pomegranate
- Raspberry
- Strawberry
- Tomato
- Watermelon
- Zucchini
## History

|Aug 31, 2023|Added Fruit Bowl.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Sep 2, 2023|Added Fruit Bowl.|
|Mar 25, 2025|0.22.1|Added Fruit Bowl as a rare reward from Garden ([[The Garden]]) visitors.|

FTX 3070 is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item and one of the items required to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv71]|[[Automaton]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%|
|[Lv67]|[[Automaton]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
### Treasure Chests
An FTX 3070 can also be found as an drop from [[Treasure Chest|treasure chests]] within the [[Precursor Remnants]]. It has a 0.34% chance to roll as a drop in common loot chests, and a 0.29% chance to roll as a drop in rare loot chests.
## Usage
### Sapphire Gemstone Crystal
Bringing an FTX 3070 and the 5 other [[Automaton]] components to [[Professor Robot]] in the [[Lost Precursor City]] rewards the player with the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Precursor Apparatus]]|None|1x [[Electron Transmitter]] 1x [[Superlite Motor]] [[FTX 3070]] 1x FTX 3070 1x [[Robotron Reflector]] 1x [[Control Switch]] 1x [[Synthetic Heart]]|Recipe: [[Control Switch]] + [[Electron Transmitter]] + FTX 3070 + [[Robotron Reflector]] + [[Superlite Motor]] + [[Synthetic Heart]] → [[Precursor Apparatus]]|
## Trivia

The Fuel Canister is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. The Fuel Canister itself is a refined material rather than a drill part, however it is used in forging [[Drills]] and Fuel Tank Drill Parts.
## Obtaining
The Fuel Canister can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It requires 2x [[Enchanted Coal Block]] and 10 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Fuel Canister]] 1 Fuel Canister
  - 2 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Coal]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Coal]]

Fuel Gabagool is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that is an upgraded form of [[Crude Gabagool]]. It can be used as a [[Pet Candies|Pet Food]] on Gabagool [[Pets]] giving 3,200 base experience. It can also be used to create special fuel for [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minions]].

|None|1x [[Very Crude Gabagool]] 8x [[Sulphuric Coal]]|Recipe: [[Sulphuric Coal]] ×8 + [[Very Crude Gabagool]] → Fuel Gabagool x8|

# Full-Jaw Fanging Kit
Aliases: Fanged
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Craftable items, Hemovibe Collection

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 5,120x (80 stacks (5,120 items total)) [[Hemovibe]] 76,800x (1,200 stacks (76,800 items total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]]) 76,800x [[Bone]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FULL_JAW_FANGING_KIT
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Fanged reforge when combined with a Melee Weapon.; Fangs! Very menacing.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Crit Chance  Attack Speed  Vitality; Fanged Bonus Every 7th melee hit on a mob deals +100%  Damage.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 13.; RARE REFORGE STONE
The Full-Jaw Fanging Kit is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Fanged]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Weapons|melee weapon]]. Its recipe is unlocked at [[Hemovibe|Hemovibe VII (7)]]. It grants [[Attack Speed]], [[Crit Chance]], [[Strength]] and [[Vitality]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Hemovibe VII (7) ([[Hemovibe]])|3x [[Enchanted Bone Block]] 3x [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 32x [[Hemoglass]]|Recipe: [[Hemoglass]] x16 ×2 + [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] ×3 + [[Enchanted Bone Block]] ×3 → Full-Jaw Fanging Kit|
## Usage
### Reforging
Full-Jaw Fanging Kit can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]] and [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Fanged]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], [[Crit Chance]], [[Attack Speed]] and [[Vitality]], which scale with the weapon's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XIII (13)]]. Weapon that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Fanged]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Fanged Bonus, which makes every 7th hit to the same enemy deal +100% more damage.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+30 Str ([[Strength]]) +1 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +2 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +3 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|+35 Str ([[Strength]]) +1 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +3 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +4 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|+40 Str ([[Strength]]) +2 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +4 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +5 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|+50 Str ([[Strength]]) +3 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +6 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +7 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|+60 Str ([[Strength]]) +4 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +9 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +8 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|+65 Str ([[Strength]]) +5 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +10 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +10 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|+75 Str ([[Strength]]) +12 Vit ([[Vitality]]) +12 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +12 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Fanged Bonus; Every 7th melee hit on a mob deals +100% damage.|Fanged Bonus; Every 7th melee hit on a mob deals +100% damage.|Fanged Bonus; Every 7th melee hit on a mob deals +100% damage.|Fanged Bonus; Every 7th melee hit on a mob deals +100% damage.|Fanged Bonus; Every 7th melee hit on a mob deals +100% damage.|Fanged Bonus; Every 7th melee hit on a mob deals +100% damage.|Fanged Bonus; Every 7th melee hit on a mob deals +100% damage.|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- This reforge stone is useful on [[Zombie Sword]] and its upgrades, and if one struggles with surviving, it can also used on Slayer Weapons, due to the granted [[Vitality]].
  - The Fanged Bonus is additive, making [[Dragon Claw|Fabled]] or [[Wither Blood|Withered]] preferable when surviving isn't an issue.

A Fungal Tip Jar is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item. It can be given to [[Sam]] to permanently unlock a tip jar that [[The Garden|Garden Visitors]] can tip [[Copper]] in.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] 128x [[Feastfungus]]|Recipe: [[Feastfungus]] x32 ×4 + [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] x32 ×3 → Fungal Tip Jar|

A Fungal Tip Jar can be given to [[Sam]] to permanently unlock a tip jar in [[The Garden]], physically located near the Visitor's Logbook.
When player accepts the [[Garden Visitors|Visitor]] request, there is a 75% chance that they will tip 2–5 [[Copper]] into the Fungal Tip Jar, which the player can later collect.

The Fungloom Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Fungy Luck]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Fungloom Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: Cave Dweller Family
- Second: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher and Forest Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Fungloom Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Fungy Luck]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Fungy Luck ([[Attributes]]); Gain a +10%–100% chance to gain an additional Chisel charge upon starting the [[Fossil Excavator]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Fungloom Shard has the following description:
- This enigmatic creature resembles a humanoid mushroom, with a cap that drips luminescent spores and tendrils that root into the cavern floor, allowing it to sense vibrations from afar.

Furball is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Itchy]] power for [[Accessories]], granting [[Speed]], [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]] and [[Attack Speed]]. It also provides +15 [[Strength]] and +15 [[Crit Damage]] as its unique power bonus.
## Obtaining
The Furball is an [[Drop Chance|7Extraordinary]] drop from the Tier III [[Sven Packmaster]] or above.
Drop Chances:
- Tier III: 0.82%
- Tier IV: 1.62%
## Requirements
The Furball requires [[Combat|Combat XX (20)]] to be used and can only be used as a Power Stone for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9 Furballs at [[Maxwell]] will permanently unlock the Itchy [[Power Stones|Power]] for [[Accessories]].

# Gabagoey Mixin
Aliases: Gabagool Mixin
Categories: Rare items, Items, Mixins, Brewing ingredients, Brewing, Potions, Slayer
[Gabagoey Mixin]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Blaze Slayer VIII (8) ([[Blaze Slayer]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Bartender]]
Buy: 150,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GABAGOEY_MIXIN
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Increases your  True Defense by 5%.; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); ☠ Requires Blaze Slayer 8.; RARE
Gabagoey Mixin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
### Purchase
A Gabagoey Mixin can be purchased from the [[Bartender]] for 150,000 [[Coins]] once the player reaches [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer VIII (8)]].
## Usage
### Brewing
A Gabagoey Mixin can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player +5% [[True Defense]]. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Gabagoey Mixin → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Gabagoey Mixin.|

## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Gecko Shard can be purchased from [[Kiara]] for 600,000 [[Coins]]. The player can purchase up to 4 per day, or up to 5 with her [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]] unlocked.
### Fusion
A Gecko Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Gecko Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Echo of Sharpening]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Echo of Sharpening ([[Attributes]]); Your Sharpening Attributes are +2%–20% stronger.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

The Gemstone Drill LT-522 is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drills|Drill]].
## Obtaining
The Gemstone Drill LT-522 can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Gemstone Drill LT-522]] 1 Gemstone Drill LT-522
  - 1 [[Ruby Drill TX-15]] 6 [[Gemstone|Fine Ruby Gemstone]] 480 (7 stacks plus 32) [[Gemstone|Flawed Ruby Gemstone]] 38,400 (600 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Ruby Gemstone]] 1 [[Fuel Canister]] 2 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Coal]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Coal]] 1 [[Drill Motor]] 10 [[Treasurite]] 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]] 3 [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 480 [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 76,800 (1,200 stacks) [[Redstone Dust]] 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]]
  - 3 [[Gemstone Mixture]] 12 [[Gemstone|Fine Jade Gemstone]] 960 (15 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Jade Gemstone]] 76,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Jade Gemstone]] 12 [[Gemstone|Fine Amber Gemstone]] 960 [[Gemstone|Flawed Amber Gemstone]] 76,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Amber Gemstone]] 12 [[Gemstone|Fine Amethyst Gemstone]] 960 [[Gemstone|Flawed Amethyst Gemstone]] 76,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Amethyst Gemstone]] 12 [[Gemstone|Fine Sapphire Gemstone]] 960 [[Gemstone|Flawed Sapphire Gemstone]] 76,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Sapphire Gemstone]] 960 [[Sludge Juice]]
## Usage
Drill:
Gemstone Drill LT-522 [[Damage]]: +75 [[Breaking Power]]: +8 [[Mining Speed]]: +300 [[Pristine]]: +1 [[Gemstone Fortune]]: +80
[Effects]
[Grants +10% ᠅ Gemstone Powder, and +800 Mining Speed when mining Gemstones.]
The Gemstone Drill LT-522 has a base 3,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]], which can be increased through a [[Drills|Fuel Tank]]. Additionally, a [[Drills|Drill Engine]] and [[Drills|Upgrade Module]] can be added to the drill through a [[Drill Anvil]] to grant further bonuses.
When breaking blocks, the Gemstone Drill LT-522's [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] will deplete. Most resources will remove 1 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] when mined; [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]], [[Gemstone|Onyx]] and [[Gemstone|Peridot]] remove 2 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead; [[Glacite]], [[Umber]] and [[Tungsten]] remove 3 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead. [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] can be regained by adding Fuel to the Drill through a [[Drill Anvil]].
This drill also gives a 10% Extra ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] which makes it good for powder mining.
### Forging
The Gemstone Drill LT-522, along with a variety of other items, can be used to forge a [[Topaz Drill KGR-12]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.
#### Forging Tree

The Gemstone Fuel Tank is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]] that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It increases the fuel capacity of [[Drills]] to 50,000 and reduces the cooldowns of [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickaxe Abilities]] by 6%.
## Obtaining
The Gemstone Fuel Tank can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]]. It takes 30s to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Gemstone Fuel Tank]] 1 Gemstone Fuel Tank

## Usage
The Gemstone Fuel Tank can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to set its fuel capacity to 50,000 and reduce the cooldowns of [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickaxe Abilities]] by 6%. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.
### Forging
It is also used in forging the [[Perfectly-Cut Fuel Tank]], which requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VIII (8)]] and takes 30 seconds.
